//Optimize for size enabled
//Strict optimizations enabled
rule ("loading pause") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        LoadingPause;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global.start, ==, False));
            Wait Until(And(Compare(Server Load, <, 180), Compare(Server Load Average, <, 180)), 10);
        Else;
            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 200), 2);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("decode text list") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        decompressTextSub;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(textItem, Char In String(Empty Array, False));
        Set Global Variable(decodeString, Null);
        While(Compare(Global.decodeString, <, Count Of(Global.decodeIn)));
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, Null);
            While(Compare(Global.decodePosition, <, String Length(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, Global.decodeString))));
                Set Global Variable(textToken, Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, String Slice(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, Global.decodeString), Global.decodePosition, 1)));
                If(Compare(Global.textToken, ==, Null));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(textGroup, Count Of(Global.textGroup), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.textItem, ==, Char In String(Empty Array, False)), Null, String Slice(Global.textItem, 0, String Length(Global.textItem))));
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Char In String(Empty Array, False));
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 53), ==, True));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Subtract(Count Of(Global.textGroup), True), Last Of(Global.textGroup));
                        Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition);
                    End;
                Else If(Compare(Global.textToken, ==, 1));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(decodeOut, Count Of(Global.decodeOut), Global.textGroup);
                    Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array);
                Else;
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 51, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 49), Round To Integer(Divide(Global.textToken, 62), Down)));
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String("{0}{1}", Global.textItem, String Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 51), Multiply(Modulo(Global.textToken, 62), 2), If-Then-Else(Compare(String Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 51), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global.textToken, 62), 2), True), 1), ==, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 50)), 1, 2))));
                    Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 53), !=, True), True);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Count Of(Global.textGroup), Global.textItem);
                    //__label_if_2__:
                End;
                Skip If(Modulo(Global.decodePosition, 12), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_3__:
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, True);
            End;
            Modify Global Variable(decodeString, Add, True);
        End;
        Skip If(Compare(Count Of(Global.decodeOut), !=, Null), True);
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Global.textGroup);
        //__label_if_4__:
    }
}

rule ("start hero counter damage") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        StartCounterMods;
    }
    actions {
        For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, Count Of((Event Player).counterMods), True);
            Stop Damage Modification(Value In Array((Event Player).counterMods, (Event Player).counterSlot));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Empty Array);
        If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 16), ==, True));
            Start Damage Modification(All Players(Team 2), Event Player, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Players In Slot(Subtract(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1)))), 3), Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Event Player))))), >, 100), 150, 100), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID);
        Else;
            For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, 3, True);
                Start Damage Modification(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Current Array Element))), ==, Evaluate Once((Event Player).counterSlot))), Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Event Player))), 3), Evaluate Once((Event Player).counterSlot))), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID);
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("hero counters: damage players do to zombies") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 16, False);
        Call Subroutine(StartCounterMods);
    }
}

rule ("hero counters: damage followers do to zombies") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4;
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7;
        (Event Player).isMonkeyBomb != True;
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 16, True);
        Call Subroutine(StartCounterMods);
    }
}

rule ("global bank interest gain every round") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.numberOfZombies <= Null;
        Global.bankPoints > Null;
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition);
        Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Multiply(Global.bankPoints, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_InterestRate, Global.bankTier)));
    }
}

rule ("global bank tier upgrade") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, First Of(Global.bankButtons)) < 1;
        Global.bankTier < Subtract(Count Of(Global.bankTiers_Cost), True);
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Global.bankPoints, >=, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, True))));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, True)));
            Modify Global Variable(bankTier, Add, True);
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You have permanently upgraded your bank to tier {0}", Global.bankTier));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
        Else;
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You need {0} points in the bank to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, True))));
    }
}

rule ("global bank withdraw button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, True)) < 1;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed);
        While(Compare(Global.bankPoints, >, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 2), 250, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), Global.bankPoints));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, (Event Player).bankStep);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, (Event Player).bankStep);
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, (Event Player).points);
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).withdrawNotif, !=, Null), True);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, withdrawNotif, 1);
            //__label_if_5__:
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("The bank is out of funds!"));
    }
}

rule ("global bank deposit button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 2)) < 1;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed);
        While(Compare((Event Player).points, >, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 2), 200, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), (Event Player).points));
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, (Event Player).bankStep);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, (Event Player).bankStep);
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, (Event Player).points);
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).depNotif, !=, Null), True);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, depNotif, 1);
            //__label_if_6__:
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You don't have enough points to deposit!"));
    }
}

rule ("property damage easter egg") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 3)) < 1;
    }

        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You have caused ${1} damage to my army!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 47)));
    }
}

rule ("credit card system") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4)) < 1;
        (Event Player).creditTier < Subtract(Count Of(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost), True);
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, True))));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, True)));
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 1000, Destination and Rate);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier, Add, True);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Limit, (Event Player).creditTier));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cashback, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cashback, (Event Player).creditTier));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Your credit card is now tier {0}", (Event Player).creditTier));
            Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold spacebar to see credit card details"));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).creditTier, ==, Null));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points buy a credit card!", Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, True)));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, True))));
    }
}

rule ("credit card interest") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).points < Null;
        Global.numberOfZombies < 1;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        (Event Player).creditTier > Null;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 48, Multiply((Event Player).points, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_InterestRate, (Event Player).creditTier)));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 48));
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 1000, Destination and Rate);
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You have incurred {0} interest on your credit card.", Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 48)));
    }
}

rule ("Reset deposit notif") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).depNotif == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold {0} to deposit: 200 at a time, then 1,000, then 10,000", Input Binding String(Button(Interact))));
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, depNotif, Null);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("Reset withdraw notif") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).withdrawNotif == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold {0} to withdraw: 250 at a time, then 1,000, then 10,000", Input Binding String(Button(Interact))));
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, withdrawNotif, Null);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("56  points loss for high roller") {
    event {
        Player Died;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    actions {
        Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Multiply((Event Player).points, 0.2));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Multiply((Event Player).points, 0.3));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, points, Round To Integer((Event Player).points, Up));
    }
}

rule ("1 Setup") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    actions {
        Disable Inspector Recording;
        Set Global Variable(roundnumber, Null);
        Set Global Variable(numberOfZombies, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 22, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 24, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 25, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 26, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 27, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 5, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, False, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, True, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, 2, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, 3, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, 4, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, 5, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(M, 6, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 125, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 120, Color(Blue));
        Set Global Variable(Difficulty, 2);
        Set Global Variable(spawntype, 3);
        Set Global Variable(experiencesystem, False);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, True);
            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        End;
        Set Global Variable(costmultiplier, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 128, -1);
    }
}

rule ("power-up effects and HUDs") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(White), First Of(Global.M), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Yellow), Value In Array(Global.M, True), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Green), Value In Array(Global.M, 2), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Violet), Value In Array(Global.M, 3), 0.1, None);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.M, 4), True, Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Orange), Value In Array(Global.M, 5), True, Position and Radius);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, True), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Double points!"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, 2), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Time Freeze"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, 4), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Insta-Kill!"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(First Of((Current Array Element).S), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Ultimate Mayhem!"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, 5), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Max Ammo!"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, 3), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Data Reset."), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Orange), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
        Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Orb, Color(Purple), Value In Array(Global.M, 6), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create HUD Text(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Compare(Value In Array((Current Array Element).S, 6), ==, 1)), Custom String("{0}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))), Null, Custom String("Fire Sale!"), Top, True, Color(White), Null, Color(Purple), Visible To and String, Visible Never);
    }
}

rule ("2 round change") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.numberOfZombies <= Null;
        Global.start == 1;
        Is True For Any(All Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).outofpsawn, ==, 1)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, Count Of(Global.omnicspawn), True);
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global.omnicspawn, Global.forindex));
        End;
        Modify Global Variable(roundnumber, Add, True);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 28, 1);
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), String("Round {0}", Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 62), Global.romanNcounter)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, False);
        Set Global Variable(roundRespawns, Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 14, Random Value In Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global.roundRespawns, perk, 5, Null);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global.roundRespawns, _extendedPlayerCollection, 15, Empty Array);
        Resurrect(Global.roundRespawns);
        Clear Status(Global.roundRespawns, Burning);
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 14), !=, Null));
            Teleport(Global.roundRespawns, Position Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 14)));
            Set Player Variable(Global.roundRespawns, doorSkipCheck, True);
        End;
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Global.roundRespawns, Hero(Ashe));
        Resurrect(Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)));
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, True);
            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 34, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 50, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 49, False);
        Set Status(All Living Players(Team 1), Null, Phased Out, 2);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), monkeyBombsLeft, 3);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 37, Add, True);
        Skip If(Compare(Global.roundnumber, >=, 12), True);
        Modify Global Variable(romanNcounter, Add, True);
        //__label_if_7__:
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 38, Random Integer(True, 5));
        Modify Global Variable(costmultiplier, Multiply, Random Real(1, 1));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 115, Random Real(1.01, 1.03));
    }
}

rule ("6 Defeat (needs to be refined for soft restart)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Is True For All(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))), Is Dead(Current Array Element)) == True;
        Is True For All(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))), Has Spawned(Current Array Element)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 29, Random Integer(False, 5));
        Destroy All Dummy Bots;
        Start Camera(All Players(Team 1), Vector(317.328, -25.644, -140.906), Vector(320.513, -24.05, -134.612), 0);
        Destroy All HUD Text;
        Destroy All Progress Bar HUD Text;
        Destroy All Effects;
        Destroy All In-World Text;
        Destroy All Icons;
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Null);
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, True);
            Set Player Variable(Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1), allowedheroes, Append To Array(Array(Hero(Ashe), Hero(D.Va)), Remove From Array((Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1)).allowedheroes, Array(Hero(Ashe), Hero(D.Va)))));
            Set Player Allowed Heroes(Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1), (Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1)).allowedheroes);
        End;
        Go To Assemble Heroes;
        Resurrect(All Players(Team 1));
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Multiply((All Players(Team 1)).points, 0.75));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), outofpsawn, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 45, False);
        Set Global Variable(roundnumber, Null);
        Set Global Variable(start, False);
        Set Global Variable(romanNcounter, Null);
        Set Global Variable(poweron, Null);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), points, Null);
        Set Global Variable(g, False);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), creditcardlimit, Null);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), creditTier, Null);
        Set Global Variable(costmultiplier, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 56, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, Count Of(Global.allDoors_Corner), True);
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_State, Global.forindex, 1);
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamA, Global.forindex, Null);
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamB, Global.forindex, Null);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(numberOfZombies, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 28, Null);
        Set Global Variable(omnicspawn, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 7, True);
            Set Global Variable At Index(M, Global.forindex, Vector(500, 25, 500));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSale, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 42, False);
        Set Global Variable(slowmoCutscene, Null);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, True);
            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        End;
        Set Global Variable(teleporterwait, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 120, Color(Blue));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 33, False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 37, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 36, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 47, Null);
        Set Global Variable(zoneEffects, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(zoneTexts, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 128, -1);
        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Null);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 41, Add, True);
    }

rule ("soft restart: a hero for players who did not pick one") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 41) > Null;
        Is Assembling Heroes == False;
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == False;

    actions {
        Wait(False, Abort When False);
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Random Value In Array(Append To Array(Array(Hero(Ashe), Hero(D.Va)), (Event Player).allowedheroes)));
    }
}

        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 52, False);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 48, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, magazines, Array(5, 5));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, 5);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, ultcharge, Array(Null, Null));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 15, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 40, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, abilitycooldown, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, heroswap, Array(Hero(Ashe), Hero(D.Va)));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, heroiconswap, Array(Hero Icon String(Hero(Ashe)), Hero Icon String(Hero(D.Va))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 45, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombOwned, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombsLeft, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, S, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, G, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, singlelives, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkinfo, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, active_wall, Empty Array);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastOutsideSpawn, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, playerRingCreated, False);

rule ("8 respawn bots") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.numberOfZombies >= 1;
        Number Of Living Players(Team 2) < Global.numberOfZombies;
        Global.Bosssequencer == Null;
        Is True For Any(All Dead Players(Team 2), Compare(Current Array Element, !=, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8))) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        Respawn(Random Value In Array(Remove From Array(All Dead Players(Team 2), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8))));
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("no going back into spawn: remember the last spot outside") {

    }

rule ("no going back into spawn") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).outofpsawn == 1;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == True;
        (Event Player).lastOutsideSpawn != Null;
    }
    actions {
        Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).lastOutsideSpawn);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck, True);
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No going back!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("497 start control") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == False;
        Global.textListsLoaded == True;
        Global.settingsViewers == Null;
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Jump)), Compare((Event Player).arcadeCodeStep, >=, 6)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 29, Random Integer(False, 5));
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global.Difficulty, >=, 4));
            Set Global Variable(buyheros, Randomized Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 55)));
            Set Global Variable(buyperks, Append To Array(Randomized Array(Array Slice(Global.buyperksHome, False, 5)), Array Slice(Global.buyperksHome, 5, Count Of(Global.buyperksHome))));
        Else;
            Set Global Variable(buyheros, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 55));
            Set Global Variable(buyperks, Global.buyperksHome);
        End;
        If(Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Power on from the start"), False, 2));
            Set Global Variable(poweron, 1);
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 52, Compare((Event Player).arcadeCodeStep, >=, 6));
        If(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 52));
            Set Global Variable(Difficulty, 1);
            Set Global Variable(difficultyStats, Array Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 30), False, 6));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), arcadeCodeStep, Null);
        Set Global Variable(roundnumber, Subtract(Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Starting wave"), 1, 1, 100, 1), True));
        Set Global Variable(start, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 126, Random Integer(True, 100));
        Set Global Variable(startlines, Random Integer(False, 29));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, False, Hero Of(Event Player));
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
        Enable Built-In Game Mode Music;
        Set Global Variable(rngbox, Random Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations));
        Start Camera(All Players(All Teams), Vector(283.872, 1.875, 118.717), Vector(282.782, 1.329, 109.203), 0);
        Create In-World Text(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Good Luck..."), Vector(282.782, 1.329, 109.203), 5, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Stop Camera(All Players(All Teams));
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Enable Hero HUD(All Players(All Teams));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 9, Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Cheats"), Custom String("Starting points"), 500, 500, 1000000, 0));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), points, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 9));
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), points2, (All Players(All Teams)).points);
        Set Player Variable(All Players(All Teams), cashback, 1);
    }
}

rule ("105 specific hero ammo") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Or(Or(Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Ability 2))), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire))), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire))) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)));
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(3, Null), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Moira)), Hero Of(Event Player)), True)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.05);
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
        End;
        If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)));
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(17, Null, 4, 10, 12), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Doomfist), Hero(D.Va), Hero(Hanzo), Hero(Sigma)), Hero Of(Event Player)), True)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.25);
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
            Skip(13);
            Skip If(Is In Alternate Form(Event Player), 3);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.05);
            Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
            //__label_if_8__:
            "pass"
            disabled Abort;
            Skip(7);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.25);
            Skip(5);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.05);
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.05);
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
            //lbl_0:
            //lbl_1:
            //lbl_2:
        Else;
            Skip(Value In Array(Array(12, Null, 4, 9), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Hero(Reaper), Hero(Cassidy), Hero(Reinhardt)), Hero Of(Event Player)), True)));
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.5);
            End;
            Skip(8);
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Skip If(Compare(Ammo(Event Player, False), ==, 6), True);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.5);
            End;
            Skip(3);
            If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Subtract, 0.25);
            End;
            //lbl_3:
            //lbl_4:
    }
}

rule ("172 FOLLOWERS  winston Omnic bomb") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Communicating(Event Player, Ultimate Status) == True;
        (Event Player).monkeyBombOwned == True;
        (Event Player).monkeyBombsLeft > Null;
        (Event Player).monkeyBombActive != True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }
    actions {
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombsLeft, Subtract, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot, Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 21, Entity Exists(Players In Slot((Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Team 1)));
        Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, (Event Player).monkeyBombSlot);
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Winston), Team 1, (Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Eye Position(Event Player), Null);
        Wait(0.03, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb, Players In Slot((Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Team 1));
        Set Player Variable((Event Player).monkeyBomb, isMonkeyBomb, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
        Attach Players((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Event Player, Vector(Empty Array, Empty Array, 2));
        Start Scaling Player((Event Player).monkeyBomb, 0.2, True);
        Start Modifying Hero Voice Lines((Event Player).monkeyBomb, 1.3, True);
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
        Detach Players((Event Player).monkeyBomb);
        Wait(0.2, Ignore Condition);
        Press Button((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Button(Jump));
        Apply Impulse((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Vector Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).monkeyBomb), 25, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion);
        Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura, Color(Orange), (Event Player).monkeyBomb, 0.5, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Last Created Entity);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Communicate((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Countdown);
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        If(Entity Exists((Event Player).monkeyBomb));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Doomfist Meteor Strike Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb), 1);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets, Players Within Radius(Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb), 4, Team 2, Surfaces And All Barriers));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, Null);
            While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22), <, Count Of((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets)));
                Damage(Value In Array((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22)), Null, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22)), Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb)), <=, 2), 150, 50));
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, Add, True);
            End;
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
            Communicate((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Goodbye);
        End;
        Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 23));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Skip If(Compare(Players In Slot((Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Team 1), !=, (Event Player).monkeyBomb), True);
        Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, (Event Player).monkeyBombSlot);
        //__label_if_9__:
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBomb, Null);
        If(And(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 21), Is Alive(Event Player)));
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, False);
    }
}

rule ("352 FOLLOWERS attack nearest zombie") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4;
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        (Event Player).isMonkeyBomb != True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False);
        While(True);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), And(And(Has Spawned(Current Array Element), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 15)), Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Current Array Element), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
            If(Compare((Event Player).followerTarget, !=, Null));
                Skip If((Event Player).followerFiring, 2);
                Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).followerTarget)), 720, To World, Direction and Turn Rate);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, True);
                //__label_if_10__:
                Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
            Else If((Event Player).followerFiring);
                Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
                Stop Facing(Event Player);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False);
            End;
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("pack a punch rocket") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        PapRocket;
    }
    actions {
        Create Homing Projectile(Pharah Rocket, Event Player, Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Facing Direction Of(Event Player)), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), To World, Damage, Team 2, (Event Player).papShotDamage, 1, 15, Ashe Dynamite Explosion Effect, Explosion Sound, (Event Player).papShotDelay, 25, 5, 0, First Of(All Living Players(Team 2)), 0.5);
    }
}

        PapStickyBomb;

    }

rule ("Pack a punch Ongoing - player") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).Packapunch >= 1;
        Or(Or(Or(Or(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), Is Using Ability 2(Event Player)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Firing Primary(Event Player)), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Baptiste)));
            If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                Add Health Pool To Player(Event Player, Health, 100, False, False);
            End;
        Else If(Is Firing Primary(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Bastion)));
                If(Is In Alternate Form(Event Player));
                    Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                    Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
                    Wait(1.5, Abort When False);
                    Loop If Condition Is True;
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Torbjörn)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 32), ==, False));
                If(Is Communicating(Event Player, Need Healing));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Empty Array);
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sphere, Color(Green), Position Of(Event Player), 0.8, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sphere, Color(Green), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 1.3, Empty Array), Position Of(Event Player)), 0.8, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                    Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ring, Color(Green), Position Of(Event Player), 3.5, Visible To);
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, dispencereffect, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 31, Evaluate Once(Position Of(Event Player)));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, 1);
                    Wait(30, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, 2);
                    Play Effect(Event Player, Debuff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 60);
                    Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
                    Destroy Effect(First Of((Event Player).dispencereffect));
                    Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player).dispencereffect, True));
                    Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player).dispencereffect, 2));
                    Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, False, Remove From Array By Index, False);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, True, Remove From Array By Index, False);
                    Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, dispencereffect, 2, Remove From Array By Index, False);
                    Wait(60, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 32, False);
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Is Firing Secondary(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Roadhog)));
                If(Compare(Ability Resource(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)), >, Null));
                    Start Heal Over Time(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team 1, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, 2, 20);
                    Create Effect(All Players(Team 1), Ring, Color(Orange), Event Player, 5, Visible To Position and Radius);
                    Wait Until(False, 5);
                    Destroy Effect(Last Created Entity);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Soldier: 76)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Echo)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapStickyBomb);
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Juno)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, Null);
                Call Subroutine(PapStickyBomb);
                Wait(0.15, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
        Else If(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Winston)));
                Set Status(Event Player, Event Player, Unkillable, 10);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Symmetra)));
                Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 10);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Reinhardt)));
                Set Status(All Living Players(Team 1), Event Player, Knocked Down, 8);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Brigitte)));
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, 1.5, True);
                Add Health Pool To Player(Event Player, Health, 1000, False, False);
                Wait(8, Ignore Condition);
                Stop Scaling Player(Event Player);
                Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Zenyatta)));
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 10.5, Team 2, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, 300);
                Wait(True, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Zarya)));
                If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                    Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), False);
                Else If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
                    Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), False);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Widowmaker)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
                If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), <, 2));
                    Wait Until(False, 4);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
                Else If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), >=, 2));
                    Wait Until(False, 16);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Lúcio)));
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team 2, Surfaces And Enemy Barriers), Event Player, Rooted, 3);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Ramattra)));
                Modify Player Variable(All Living Players(Team 1), points, Add, 1000);
                Modify Player Variable(All Living Players(Team 1), magazines, Add, 5);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Genji)));
                If(Is Firing Primary(Event Player));
                    Create Projectile(Reinhardt Fire Strike, Event Player, Eye Position(Event Player), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), To World, Damage, Team 2, 300, 1, 0, Bad Explosion, Explosion Sound, 0, 10, 5, 0, 0, 0);
                End;
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Orisa)));
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, 2, True);
                Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, 1000);
                Wait Until(False, 8);
                Start Scaling Player(Event Player, True, True);
                Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, (Event Player).damagevariable);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Kiriko)));
                Set Move Speed(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 15, Team 2, Off), 50);
                Wait(8, Ignore Condition);
                If(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <=, 6));
                    Set Move Speed(All Players(Team 2), Add(52, Multiply(8, Global.roundnumber)));
                Else If(Compare(Global.roundnumber, >, 6));
                    Set Move Speed(All Players(Team 2), 100);
                End;
            End;
        Else If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
            Skip If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), !=, Hero(Sojourn)), True);
            Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
            //__label_if_11__:
        Else If(Is Using Ability 2(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Tracer)));
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Evaluate Once(Position Of(Event Player)), 5, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Stunned, 4);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Sombra)));
                Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, 1);
                Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(D.Va)));
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 400);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, Null);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Lifeweaver)));
                Add Health Pool To Player(Players in View Angle(Event Player, Team 1, 5), Health, 100, False, False);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Pharah)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDamage, 500);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, papShotDelay, 0.2);
                Call Subroutine(PapRocket);
                Wait(0.2, Abort When False);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("Pack a Punch - Damage dealt") {
    event {
        Player Dealt Damage;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Array Contains(Array(Hero(Soldier: 76), Hero(Cassidy), Hero(Sigma), Hero(Junkrat), Hero(Moira), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Doomfist)), Hero Of(Event Player)) == True;
        (Event Player).Packapunch >= 1;
        Or(Or(Or(Or(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), Is Using Ability 2(Event Player)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Firing Primary(Event Player)), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Cassidy)));
            If(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)));
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 3, Team 2, Off), Null, 420);
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(White), Victim, 3);
                Wait(0.45, Ignore Condition);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Sigma)));
            Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 5, Team 2, Off), Null, 200);
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Victim, 5);
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Junkrat)));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Orange), Victim, 8);
            Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 8, Team 2, Off), Null, 300);
        Else If(Is Using Ultimate(Event Player));
            If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Moira)));
                Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Rooted, 4);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Moira)));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Victim, 5);
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 5, Team 2, Off), Attacker, 400);
            Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Brigitte)));
                If(Has Status(Victim, Stunned));
                    Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
                End;
            Else If(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player));
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Doomfist)));
                    Set Status(Victim, Event Player, Knocked Down, 4);
    }
}

rule ("give pack a punch a sound") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).Packapunch >= 1;
        Server Load < 220;
        Or(Is Firing Primary(Event Player), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Is Firing Primary(Event Player));
            If(Array Contains(Mapped Array(String Split(Custom String("0000"), First Of(Null)), Hero(Cassidy)), Hero Of(Event Player)));
                Call Subroutine(PaPsound);
            End;
        Else If(Is Firing Secondary(Event Player));
            If(Array Contains(Mapped Array(String Split(Custom String("0000"), First Of(Null)), Hero(Cassidy)), Hero Of(Event Player)));
                Call Subroutine(PaPsound);
    }
}

rule ("Pack a Punch - final b­low bonuses (Venture, Mauga, Sigma)") {
    event {
        Player Dealt Final Blow;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Array Contains(Array(Hero(Venture), Hero(Mauga), Hero(Sigma)), Hero Of(Event Player)) == True;
        (Event Player).Packapunch >= 1;
        Or(Or(Or(Or(Or(Is Using Ability 1(Event Player), Is Using Ability 2(Event Player)), Is Using Ultimate(Event Player)), Is Firing Primary(Event Player)), Is Firing Secondary(Event Player)), Is Meleeing(Event Player)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Venture)));
            If(Is Meleeing(Event Player));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(Blue), Victim, 5);
                Damage(Players Within Radius(Victim, 5, Team 2, Off), Null, 800);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Mauga)));
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), False);
            Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), False);
        Else If(Compare(Hero Of(Attacker), ==, Hero(Sigma)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, 50);
    }
}

rule ("pack a punch ability") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        If-Then-Else(Array Contains((Event Player).endingHeroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 3, Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 5)) != (Event Player).papAppliedTier;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, papAppliedTier, If-Then-Else(Array Contains((Event Player).endingHeroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 3, Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 5)));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, damagevariable, Value In Array(Array(100, 250, 500, 1000), (Event Player).papAppliedTier));
        Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, (Event Player).damagevariable);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch, (Event Player).papAppliedTier);
    }
}

rule ("pack a punch: followers") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4;
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7;
        (Event Player).isMonkeyBomb != True;
        (Event Player).damagevariable != Value In Array(Array(100, 250, 500, 1000), Max(False, (Players In Slot(Subtract(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1)).papAppliedTier));
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch, Max(False, (Players In Slot(Subtract(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1)).papAppliedTier));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, damagevariable, Value In Array(Array(100, 250, 500, 1000), (Event Player).Packapunch));
        Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, (Event Player).damagevariable);
    }
}

rule ("stop jetpack cat from flying too long") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        Jetpack Cat;
    }
    conditions {
        Altitude Of(Event Player) >= 1.5;
        Altitude Of(Event Player) < 100;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(3, Abort When False);
        Start Accelerating(Event Player, Down, 100, 20, To World, Direction Rate and Max Speed);
        Wait Until(Compare(Altitude Of(Event Player), <=, 1.5), 5);
        Disallow Button(Event Player, Button(Jump));
        Stop Accelerating(Event Player);
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Allow Button(Event Player, Button(Jump));
    }
}

rule ("apply fall damage") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).enablefalldamage == 1;
        Is On Ground(Event Player) == True;
        (Event Player).fall_Damage_Speed < -15;
    }
    actions {
        Damage(Event Player, Null, Multiply(Divide(Multiply(Divide(Multiply(Absolute Value((Event Player).fall_Damage_Speed), Max Health(Event Player)), 200), Absolute Value((Event Player).fall_Damage_Speed)), 8), 3));
    }
}

rule ("Find fall speed for fall damage") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).enablefalldamage == 1;
        Altitude Of(Event Player) < 1;
        Is In Air(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, fall_Damage_Speed, Vertical Speed Of(Event Player));
    }
}

rule ("enable/toggle fall damage regular") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is In Air(Event Player) == True;
        Is Using Ability 1(Event Player) == False;
        Is Using Ability 2(Event Player) == False;
        Is Using Ultimate(Event Player) == False;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Add(First Of(Global.heroFallDamageTable), (Event Player).fallDamageOffsetTable), ==, 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, enablefalldamage, 1);
        Else;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, enablefalldamage, False);
    }
}

    }

rule ("393 POINTS Omnic kill (final hit)") {
    event {
        Player Dealt Final Blow;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killKind, If-Then-Else(Is Meleeing(Event Player), 2, If-Then-Else(Event Was Critical Hit, 1, Null)));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killPoints, Multiply(Add(Value In Array(Array(50, 100, 60), (Event Player).killKind), Multiply((Event Player).heropoints, (Event Player).H)), Raise To Power(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 115), Subtract(Global.roundnumber, True))));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, (Event Player).killPoints);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 47, Add, (Event Player).killPoints);
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), Value In Array(Array(1000, 1000, 250), (Event Player).killKind), Destination and Rate);
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).H, ==, 1), True);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(Value In Array(Array(100, 200, 120), (Event Player).killKind), (Event Player).H));
        //__label_if_12__:
        Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, xp, (Event Player).currentheroxp, Add, Value In Array(Array(5, 10, 6), (Event Player).killKind));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.difficultyStats, 5), If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.5, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Tank Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.25, 1))), 2)));
    }
}

    event {
        Player Earned Elimination;

        All;
    }

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killKind, If-Then-Else(Is Meleeing(Event Player), 2, If-Then-Else(Event Was Critical Hit, 1, Null)));
        If(Array Contains(All Damage Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints, Multiply(15, Add((Event Player).killKind, True)));
        Else If(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints, Multiply(5, Add((Event Player).killKind, True)));
        Else If(Array Contains(All Tank Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, heropoints, Multiply(10, Add((Event Player).killKind, True)));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, killPoints, Multiply(Add(Value In Array(Array(40, 80, 70), (Event Player).killKind), Multiply((Event Player).heropoints, (Event Player).H)), Raise To Power(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 115), Subtract(Global.roundnumber, True))));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, (Event Player).killPoints);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 47, Add, (Event Player).killPoints);
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).killKind, !=, 2), True);
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 250, Destination and Rate);
        //__label_if_13__:
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).H, ==, 1), True);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(Value In Array(Array(80, 160, 70), (Event Player).killKind), (Event Player).H));
        //__label_if_14__:
        Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, xp, (Event Player).currentheroxp, Add, Value In Array(Array(5, 10, 7), (Event Player).killKind));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.difficultyStats, 5), If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Support Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.5, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(All Tank Heroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 1.25, 1))), 2)));
    }

rule ("toggle camera toggle") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).cameratoggle, ==, 2));
            Wait(True, Abort When False);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cameratoggle, False);
            Stop Camera(Event Player);
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).cameratoggle, ==, False));
            Wait(True, Abort When False);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cameratoggle, 1);
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).cameratoggle, ==, 1));
            Wait(True, Abort When False);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cameratoggle, 2);
    }
}

rule ("26 Revive Player system") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Is True For Any(Global.Died, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), <, 4)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget, Players In Slot(Index Of Array Value(Global.Died, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.Died, Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player)))), Team 1));
        Chase Player Variable Over Time((Event Player).reviveTarget, sea, 4, Add(2, Multiply(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 23), 2)), Destination and Duration);
        Wait(Add(2, Multiply(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 23), 2)), Abort When False);
        Resurrect((Event Player).reviveTarget);
        Teleport((Event Player).reviveTarget, Event Player);
        Set Player Variable((Event Player).reviveTarget, doorSkipCheck, True);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, Add, True);
        Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Slot Of((Event Player).reviveTarget), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Multiply(100, (Event Player).H));
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 1000, Destination and Rate);
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).H, !=, 1), True);
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).H, ==, 1), True);
        //lbl_0:
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, False);
        //lbl_1:
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 118, Add, Multiply(100, (Event Player).H));
        Set Status((Event Player).reviveTarget, Null, Phased Out, 5);
        Set Max Health((Event Player).reviveTarget, 100);
        Stop Chasing Player Variable((Event Player).reviveTarget, sea);
        Set Player Variable((Event Player).reviveTarget, sea, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, reviveTarget, Null);
    }
}

rule ("hero swap subroutine") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        Subheroswap;
    }
    actions {
        Preload Hero(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player).heroswap, First Of((Event Player).swapvar)));
        Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 40));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, True), Health(Event Player));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 40, Global._arrayConstructor);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, ultcharge, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, True), Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player));
        Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 15));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, True), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 5));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 15, Global._arrayConstructor);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, 2), Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, 3), Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, 4), Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire)));
        Press Button(Event Player, Button(Reload));
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, magazines, Value In Array((Event Player).swapvar, True), (Event Player).currentmagazines);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentswap, First Of((Event Player).swapvar));
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, Value In Array((Event Player).magazines, First Of((Event Player).swapvar)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 44, Value In Array((Event Player).ultcharge, First Of((Event Player).swapvar)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, Value In Array(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 15), First Of((Event Player).swapvar)));
    }
}

rule ("switch between 2 heros (needs ultimates, followers, ammo, systems working)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch)) == True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 200);
        Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player);
        If(Compare((Event Player).currentswap, ==, False));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(1, Null, Null, 1, 2));
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap);
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).currentswap, ==, 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(Null, 1, 3, 4, 5));
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap);
        End;
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player).heroswap, (Event Player).currentswap));
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Health(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 40), First Of((Event Player).swap)));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 2)));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 3)));
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 4)));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 44));
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2));
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
    }
}

        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        (Event Player).outofpsawn == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 13, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 45), First Of(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), And(And(And(Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)), Compare(Current Array Element, !=, Event Player)), Compare((Current Array Element).outofpsawn, ==, 1)), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element))))), Null));
        If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 13), !=, Null));
            Teleport(Event Player, Position Of(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 13)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck, True);
        Else;
            Call Subroutine(getoutofspawn);
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 45, True);
    }
}

    }
