rule ("save ramattra coins") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SaveRamattraCoins;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex, Index Of Array Value(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 12), Custom String("{0}", Event Player)));
        If(Compare((Event Player).coinNameIndex, <, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinNameIndex, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 12)));
            Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 12));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, (Event Player).coinNameIndex, Custom String("{0}", Event Player));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 12, Global._arrayConstructor);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 13));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, (Event Player).coinNameIndex, (Event Player).ramattraCoins);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 13, Global._arrayConstructor);
    }
}

rule ("spend ramattra coins") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SpendRamattraCoins;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).coinUses, >, Null));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, ramattraCoins, Subtract, Multiply((Event Player).coinUses, Global.pricePerUse));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses, Null);
            Call Subroutine(SaveRamattraCoins);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("finish purchase feedback") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.05, Ignore Condition);
        Set Move Speed(Event Player, False);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
    }
}

rule ("202 Purchase Follower action") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).monkeyBombActive != True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(True, Abort When False);
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Global.hero), True);
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.i)), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.i), !=, Null)));
                If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)));
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 45, Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.i));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest));
                    Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
                    Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                    Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy);
                    Skip If((Event Player).firstFollowerShown, 4);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown, True);
                    Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                    Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up);
                    Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote);
                    //__label_if_15__:
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i);
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).messageSpot)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 65), (Event Player).messageSpot)));
                End;
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("Purchase Mystery Follower action") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).monkeyBombActive != True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
        Global.start == 1;
        Global.rngbox != Null;
        Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global.rngbox), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), Is True For Any(Global.fireSaleBoxes, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2)))) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(True, Abort When False);
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(First Of(Global.herobuycosts), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 45, Random Value In Array(Global.hero));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(First Of(Global.herobuycosts), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(First Of(Global.herobuycosts), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest));
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
            Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy);
            Skip If((Event Player).firstFollowerShown, 4);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown, True);
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up);
            Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote);
            //__label_if_16__:
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(First Of(Global.hero)), First Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 65))));
    }
}

rule ("229 buy perks and power activate") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Array Slice(Global.buyperks, False, 6), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, 6, True);
            If(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Global.i)), <, 2));
                If(Compare(Global.poweron, ==, 1));
                    If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)));
                        If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).perk, Global.i), <, 3));
                            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest));
                            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, Global.i, Add, True);
                            Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i);
                            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.i, ==, 5), 5, 3));
                            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 121), (Event Player).messageSpot)));
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up);
                            Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote);
                            Break;
                        Else;
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You already have the max tier!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                            Break;
                        End;
                    Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i)));
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO perks!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                        Break;
                    End;
                Else;
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} The power is not ON!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                    Break;
                End;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("connect power") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 6)) < 2;
        Global.poweron == Null;
    }
    actions {
        Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
        Destroy Icon(Global.powerconnected);
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Remove From Array By Value, Global.powerconnected);
        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Null);
        Set Global Variable(poweron, 1);
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("The power is now ON!"));
        If(Compare(Number Of Living Players(Team 2), >, Null));
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} Prepare for death!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Else;
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} Nice timing", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 2), _extendedPlayerCollection, 20, True);
    }
}

rule ("202 Purchase hero actions") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        For Global Variable(idx, 0, Count Of(Global.buyheros), True);
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.idx)), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.idx), !=, Null)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot, Global.idx);
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)));
                    If(And(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)), Compare((Event Player).currentmagazines, <, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10))));
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest));
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Destination and Rate);
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10));
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Magazines refilled!", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot))));
                        Break;
                    Else;
                        If(Compare((Event Player).currentmagazines, >=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10)));
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You already have full magazines!"));
                        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2)));
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO magazines!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                        End;
                    End;
                Else;
                    If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)));
                        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot));
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, Null);
                        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot));
                        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)));
                        If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
                            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
                        End;
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest));
                        Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
                        Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Destination and Rate);
                        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2));
                        Skip If(Array Contains((Event Player).allowedheroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), True);
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player));
                        //__label_if_17__:
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10));
                    Else;
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 64), (Event Player).buySpot)));
                    End;
                    Break;
                End;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("buy the ending") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Distance Between(Event Player, Vector(-101.303, -150.36, -17.831)) < 2;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, 50000));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Multiply(50000, (Event Player).cashback));
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 50000, Destination and Rate);
            Set Player Variable At Index(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))), _extendedPlayerCollection, 25, True);
            Set Global Variable(slowmoCutscene, 40);
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
            Set Global Variable(slowmoCutscene, Null);
            Small Message(All Players(Team 1), Custom String("{0} You really thought this was going to end?", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
            Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), singlelives, 3);
            Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), perk, 2, Null);
            Kill(All Players(All Teams), Null);
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} HA, good luck escaping...", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
    }
}

rule ("53  mystery box") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Global.start == 1;
        Global.rngbox != Null;
        Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global.rngbox), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), Is True For Any(Global.fireSaleBoxes, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2)))) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), (Event Player).creditcardlimit)));
            If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5);
                Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            End;
            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5);
            Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, First Of(Random Value In Array(Randomized Array(All Heroes))));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, Null);
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
            Resurrect(Event Player);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Multiply(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), (Event Player).cashback));
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Destination and Rate);
            If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235));
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            End;
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Hero Of(Event Player));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Hero Icon String(Hero Of(Event Player)));
            Skip If(Array Contains((Event Player).allowedheroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), True);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player));
            //__label_if_19__:
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10));
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, Add, True);
            Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
            Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 34), <, Random Integer(6, 15)), 2);
            Set Global Variable(rngbox, Random Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, False);
            //lbl_0:
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), (Event Player).creditcardlimit)));
            Big Message(Event Player, Custom String("Come back when you've got some money buddy!"));
    }
}

    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;

    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 37, Add(Multiply((Event Player).currentheroxp, 1000), Value In Array((Event Player).XPlevel, (Event Player).currentheroxp)));
        Set Secondary Fire Enabled(Event Player, And(Or(Compare(Global.experiencesystem, ==, False), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).XPlevel, (Event Player).currentheroxp), >=, 5)), Compare((Event Player).currentmagazines, >, Null)));

        Set Ultimate Ability Enabled(Event Player, Or(Compare(Global.experiencesystem, ==, False), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).XPlevel, (Event Player).currentheroxp), >=, 20)));
    }
}

rule ("Ramattra coins: say I need healing to use one") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
        Global.start == 1;
        Is Communicating(Event Player, Need Healing) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice, If-Then-Else(Or(Or(Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), Is True For Any(Global.bossbuypositions, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2))), And(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Global.rngbox, !=, Null)), Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global.rngbox), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), Is True For Any(Global.fireSaleBoxes, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2)))))), 1, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 5)), <, 2), 5, If-Then-Else(Is True For Any(Array Slice(Global.buyperks, False, 5), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), 3, Null))));
        If(Compare((Event Player).coinPrice, ==, Null));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).coinUses, >=, 3));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Already using 3 Ramattra coins here (an eighth of the price)"));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).ramattraCoins, >=, Multiply(Add((Event Player).coinUses, True), (Event Player).coinPrice)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses, Add, True);
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Ramattra coins used: {1}. Price x {2}. Buy as usual", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), (Event Player).coinUses, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Not enough Ramattra coins: this costs {0} a use (you have {1})", (Event Player).coinPrice, (Event Player).ramattraCoins));
    }
}

rule ("arcade: Soldier, aimbot and overhead camera") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 52) == True;
        Global.start == 1;
        (Event Player).outofpsawn == 1;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }
    actions {
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Hero(Soldier: 76));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, False, Hero(Soldier: 76));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, False, Hero Icon String(Hero(Soldier: 76)));
        Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))))), 800, To World, Direction and Turn Rate);
        Start Transforming Throttle(Event Player, 1, 1, Vector(-0.109, Empty Array, 0.994));
        While(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1));
            If(Is Alive(Event Player));
                Start Camera(Event Player, Subtract(Ray Cast Hit Position(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(0.778, 14.866, -7.117)), Null, Event Player, False), Vector(0.024, 0.451, -0.216)), Eye Position(Event Player), 20);
                Skip If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Hero(Soldier: 76)), True);
                Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), 40));
                //__label_if_22__:
            End;
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Stop Facing(Event Player);
        Stop Transforming Throttle(Event Player);
    }
}

rule ("137  get out of spawn bots") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == True;
        Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches) > Null;
    }
    actions {
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, 9999);
        Wait(Add(0.25, Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.05)), Abort When False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick, Random Integer(False, Subtract(Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), True)));
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), >, Null));
            Set Global Variable At Index(globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick, Max(False, Subtract(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 103))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, index, (Event Player).breachPick);
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index), <=, Null));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index, Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index, Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(True, Divide(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index)), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), >, Null), True);
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, (Event Player).breachPick), 3);
            //__label_if_23__:
            Wait(1.5, Abort When False);
        Else;
            Start Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(Team 1), True, Custom Color(Random Integer(False, 255), Random Integer(False, 255), Random Integer(False, 255), 255), Default);
            Clear Status(Event Player, Rooted);
            Teleport(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, (Event Player).breachPick));
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("barricade repair by players") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 86, 25);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick, Index Of Array Value(Global.globalActiveBreaches, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Distance Between(Current Array Element, Event Player)))));
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), <, 100));
            Set Global Variable At Index(globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick, Min(100, Add(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 86))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, index_0, (Event Player).breachPick);
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index_0), <=, Null));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index_0, Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index_0, Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(True, Divide(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index_0), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index_0)), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 50);
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, 10);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 1000, Destination and Rate);
        End;
        Wait(True, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("rebuild barricade effects") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        RebuildBreachEffects;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 96)), True);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 96), Global.botspawnindex));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(color, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), True);
            Set Global Variable(index, Global.botspawnindex);
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, Global.index), <=, Null));
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Global.index, Color(Black));
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, Global.index, Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(True, Divide(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, Global.index), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, Global.index)), 100), 0, 255));
            End;
            Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura, Value In Array(Global.color, Evaluate Once(Global.botspawnindex)), Add(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex), Multiply(1.5, Up)), True, Visible To and Color);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura Sound, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex), 50, Visible To and Color);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 96, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("get nearest nodes for spawning") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 103, 25);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 104, 8);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 105, 35);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 109, 15);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 91, 8);
        Set Global Variable(teamPlayers, Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), And(Compare((Current Array Element).outofpsawn, ==, 1), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element)))));
        If(And(Compare(Count Of(Global.teamPlayers), >, Null), Compare(Count Of(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes), >, Null)));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Add(Left, Right));
            For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global.teamPlayers), True);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Add, Position Of(Value In Array(Global.teamPlayers, Global.botspawnindex)));
            End;
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Divide, Count Of(Global.teamPlayers));
            If(Or(Or(Compare(Global.averagePosition, ==, Null), Compare(Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), ==, Null)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 106), Global.averagePosition), >, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 109))));
                Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 106));
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Empty Array);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Empty Array);
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), True);
                    If(Compare(Distance Between(Global.averagePosition, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex)), <, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 105)));
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex));
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, Global.botspawnindex));
                    End;
                End;
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Compare(Distance Between(Global.averagePosition, Current Array Element), <, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 105))));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global.candidates, And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Closest Player To(Current Array Element, Team 1)), >=, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 104)), Not(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107), Current Array Element)))));
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 92, Min(Count Of(Global.candidates), Subtract(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 91), Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107)))));
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 92), True);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 93, Random Value In Array(Global.candidates));
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 93));
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, >, 5), Null, 100));
                    Modify Global Variable(candidates, Remove From Array By Value, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 93));
                End;
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107));
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 108));
                Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects);
            End;
        End;
        Wait(5, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

    }

rule ("each bot will be random") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        totalrandomspawn;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Null);
        Call Subroutine(wavevoicecall);
        Call Subroutine(setbotamount);
        For Global Variable(waveSlot, 0, Global.botamount, True);
            Wait Until(Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False), 99999);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.waveSlot, Team 2), ==, Null));
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 39, Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(False, 37)));
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 39), Team 2, Global.waveSlot, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
                Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global.waveSlot, Slot Of(Players In Slot(Global.waveSlot, Team 2)));
            End;
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Global.waveSlot);
            Wait(0.75, Abort When False);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("518 spawn bots subroutine") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        wavespawn;
    }
    actions {
        Call Subroutine(wavevoicecall);
        Wait Until(Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False), 99999);
        Skip If(Compare(Players In Slot(False, Team 2), !=, Null), True);
        Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 7), Global.g), Team 2, False, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
        //__label_if_25__:
        Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, False, Slot Of(Players In Slot(False, Team 2)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Players In Slot(False, Team 2));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Null);
        Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
        Call Subroutine(setbotamount);
        For Global Variable(waveSlot, 1, Add(Global.botamount, True), 2);
            Wait Until(Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False), 99999);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.waveSlot, Team 2), ==, Null));
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Global.bot1spawn, Global.g), Team 2, Global.waveSlot, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
                Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global.waveSlot, Slot Of(Players In Slot(Global.waveSlot, Team 2)));
            End;
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Global.waveSlot);
            Wait(0.75, Abort When False);
            If(Compare(Add(Global.waveSlot, True), <=, Global.botamount));
                Wait Until(Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False), 99999);
                If(Compare(Players In Slot(Add(Global.waveSlot, True), Team 2), ==, Null));
                    Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 15), Global.g), Team 2, Add(Global.waveSlot, True), First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Add(Global.waveSlot, True), Slot Of(Players In Slot(Add(Global.waveSlot, True), Team 2)));
                End;
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 40, Add(Global.waveSlot, True));
                Wait(0.75, Abort When False);
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("519 Spawn wave director") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.roundnumber >= 1;
        Global.numberOfZombies >= 4;
        Is Game In Progress == True;
    }
    actions {
        Skip If(Compare(Global.spawntype, >=, 3), True);
        Set Global Variable(g, False);
        //__label_if_26__:
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(15, Null, 4, 8, 12), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(False, 1, 2, 3), Global.spawntype), True)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Brigitte)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Global.bot1spawn);
        Skip(11);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Torbjörn)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Global.bot1spawn);
        Skip(7);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <, 5), Null, Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(False, 37))));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(False, 37)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(False, 37)));
        Skip(3);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 7, Array(Null, Hero(Doomfist), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Winston), Hero(Roadhog), Hero(Junker Queen), Hero(Zenyatta), Hero(Mauga), Hero(Sombra), Hero(Mei), Hero(Reaper), Hero(Ramattra)));
        Set Global Variable(bot1spawn, Array(Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Moira), Hero(Lúcio), Hero(Torbjörn), Hero(Roadhog), Hero(Moira), Hero(Lúcio), Hero(Mercy), Hero(Junker Queen), Hero(Tracer), Hero(Doomfist)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 15, Array(Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Reinhardt), Hero(Winston), Hero(Genji), Hero(Winston), Hero(Genji), Hero(Brigitte), Hero(Hanzo), Hero(Mauga), Hero(D.Va), Hero(Echo), Hero(Sombra)));
        //lbl_0:
        //lbl_1:
        //lbl_2:
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4));
            Call Subroutine(totalrandomspawn);
        Else;
            Call Subroutine(wavespawn);
    }
}

rule ("Zombie AI settings and server load gate") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Set Global Variable(zombieLoadLimit, Workshop Setting Integer(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Server load limit for zombie AI extras"), 200, 50, 250, 0));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 20, Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Zombies pathfind when stuck or you are above them"), True, 1));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 21, Workshop Setting Toggle(Custom String("Pathfinding"), Custom String("Zombies run faster when far away"), True, 2));
        Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, True);
        While(True);
            If(Global.zombieExtrasAllowed);
                If(Or(Compare(Server Load Average, >, Global.zombieLoadLimit), Compare(Server Load, >, Add(Global.zombieLoadLimit, 60))));
                    Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, False);
                End;
            Else If(And(Compare(Server Load Average, <, Subtract(Global.zombieLoadLimit, 30)), Compare(Server Load, <, Global.zombieLoadLimit)));
                Set Global Variable(zombieExtrasAllowed, True);
            End;
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget, Null);

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, Null);

    }

    }
}

rule ("Zombie chase: straight at you, pathfinding only when stuck or you are above") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == False;
        And(Array Contains(Global.bossBots, Event Player), Compare(Event Player, !=, Global.bossBody)) == False;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).p_T, ==, Null)), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
        Skip If(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, !=, Null), True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
        //__label_if_28__:
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 10, If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, ==, Null), 9999, Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).zombieTarget)));
        If(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, ==, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, Null);
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
        Else;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance, Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(True, Empty Array, True)), Multiply(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget), Vector(True, Empty Array, True))));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieHeightDifference, Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget)), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))));
            If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, ==, 1));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 9, And(Compare(Absolute Value((Event Player).zombieHeightDifference), <, 1.2), Or(Compare((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, <, 3), And(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), >, 3), Is In Line of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Up), Add(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget), Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 20), Global.pfMapReady), Not(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 9))), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), <, 20)), Compare(Server Load, <, Add(Global.zombieLoadLimit, 60))));
            Else;
                If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Horizontal Speed Of(Event Player), <, 1.5), Compare((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, >, 3)), Not((Event Player).zombieBusy)), Is On Ground(Event Player)), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Hacked))));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, Add, True);
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, Null);
                End;
                If(Compare(Absolute Value((Event Player).zombieHeightDifference), >, 1.8));
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, Add, True);
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, Null);
                End;
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 20), Global.pfMapReady), Global.zombieExtrasAllowed), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), >, 1.5)), Or(Compare((Event Player).zombieStuckChecks, >=, 3), Compare((Event Player).zombieAboveChecks, >=, 2))));
            End;
            If(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 8));
                If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, !=, 1));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 1);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed);
                    Call Subroutine(pfLookAtDestination);
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget));
            Else;
                If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, !=, 2));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 2);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, Null);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, Null);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null);
                    Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).zombieTarget)), 400, To World, Direction and Turn Rate);
                End;
                Skip If((Event Player).pfIsPathfinding, True);
                Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player), Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget)), 1, To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude);
                //__label_if_29__:
            End;
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 21), Add(True, Min(Multiply(Max(Subtract((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, 15), False), 0.04), 0.6)), 1));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, Divide(Round To Integer(Multiply((Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost, 10), Down), 10));
            If(And(Not((Event Player).zombieBusy), Compare(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global.roundnumber)), 128), (Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost), !=, (Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed, Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global.roundnumber)), 128), (Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 4, Divide((Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed, 100));
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, (Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed);
            End;
        End;
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Global.zombieExtrasAllowed, 0.25, 0.5), Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("Zombie stop moving when dead") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dead(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, Null);
        Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Decompress】Decompress map") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.pfDecompress == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False);
        Set Global Variable(pfcompressionTable, Custom String(" {0}", Global.decodeTable));
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(First Of(Global.pfDecompress)), True);
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1)), 6);
                Modify Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Append To Array, Vector(Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Global.pfI2)), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, True)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 2))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 3)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 4))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 5)))), 524288), 100)));
                Skip If(Modulo(Global.pfI2, 24), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_30__:
            End;
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, Null);
        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, True)), True);
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, True), Global.pfI1)), True);
                Set Global Variable(pfnext, Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, True), Global.pfI1), Global.pfI2)));
                If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 17), ==, Null));
                    If(Not(Global.pfnext));
                        Modify Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Append To Array, Array(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors));
                        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array);
                    Else;
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 1);
                        Modify Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Append To Array, Array(Array(Subtract(Global.pfnext, True), Empty Array)));
                    End;
                Else If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 17), ==, 1));
                    If(Not(Global.pfnext));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, Null);
                    Else;
                        Set Global Variable At Index(pfcurrentNeighbors, Subtract(Count Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors), True), Array(First Of(Last Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors)), Append To Array(Value In Array(Last Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors), True), Subtract(Global.pfnext, True))));
                    End;
                End;
                Skip If(Modulo(Global.pfI2, 20), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_31__:
            End;
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes), True);
            Modify Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Append To Array, Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide(Global.pfI1, 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global.pfI1, 42), 3), 2))));
            Skip If(Modulo(Global.pfI1, 20), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_32__:
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 18, Mapped Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Index));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Add(Last Of(Sorted Array(Global.pfNodeComponent, Current Array Element)), True), True);
            Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 19));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Global.pfI1, Filtered Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 18), Compare(Value In Array(Global.pfNodeComponent, Current Array Element), ==, Global.pfI1)));
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Global._arrayConstructor);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, True);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Update node meta") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfUpdateNodeMeta;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, True, Total Time Elapsed);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingFromPosition, Position Of(Event Player));
    }
}

rule ("☆【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Walk to destination") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfWalkToDestination;
    }
    actions {
        Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player), If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1), (Event Player).pfDestination, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex))), And(Compare(Distance Between(Position Of(Event Player), If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1), (Event Player).pfDestination, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex))), >, 0.4), Or(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, !=, -1), Not(Or(Not((Event Player).pfSavedTarget), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfDestination, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget), >, 5))))), To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core】Update & Execute") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfDestination == True;
        Or(Not((Event Player).pfSavedTarget), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfDestination, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget), >, 5)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Skip If(Global.pfMapReady, True);
        Wait Until(Global.pfMapReady, 99999);
        //__label_if_33__:
        Wait(Random Real(0, 0.3), Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget, (Event Player).pfDestination);
        If(Or(Not((Event Player).pfIsPathfinding), Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 18), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Element), Position Of(Event Player))), False, 5), Not(Is In Line of Sight(Add(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Element), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Add(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfGoalNode, First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 19), Value In Array(Global.pfNodeComponent, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex)), Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Element), (Event Player).pfSavedTarget)), False, 5), Not(Is In Line of Sight(Add(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Element), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Add((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)))));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding, True);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex));
        Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
        Call Subroutine(pfWalkToDestination);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Read next hop char") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfReadNextHopChar;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopChar, Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 2), Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfHopPosition, 128), Down)), Modulo((Event Player).pfHopPosition, 128))));
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Look up next hop (Floyd-Warshall, RLE base 1024)") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfLookupNextHop;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), Down)), Multiply(Modulo((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), 3))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), 3), True)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, (Event Player).pfGoalNode);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, Null);
        Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 5), >, Modulo((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128)));
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, Subtract, Add(Modulo((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128), True));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add, True);
            Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, Add, True);
            Skip If(Compare(Modulo(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 6), 25), !=, Null), True);
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
            //__label_if_34__:
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 7, Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128), Down));
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Progress event player") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfProgressEventPlayer;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, (Event Player).pfGoalNode));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Empty Array);
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
        Else;
            Call Subroutine(pfLookupNextHop);
            If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 7), >=, Count Of(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_neighbors, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex))));
                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0}: no path from node {1} to node {2}.", Event Player, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, (Event Player).pfGoalNode));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Empty Array);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
            Else;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfPreviousNode, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, First Of(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_neighbors, (Event Player).pfPreviousNode), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 7))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Value In Array(First Of(Filtered Array(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_neighbors, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex), Compare(First Of(Current Array Element), ==, (Event Player).pfPreviousNode))), True));
            End;
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1), (Event Player).pfDestination, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex)));
        Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Recalibrate") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfRecalibrate;
    }
    actions {
        Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding, False);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core】Watch progress (checked every 4 ticks)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfIsPathfinding == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, Null);
        While((Event Player).pfIsPathfinding);
            If(And(And(And((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, !=, -1)), Compare(Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(True, Empty Array, True)), Multiply((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Vector(True, Empty Array, True))), <=, Multiply(0.6, Max(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 4), True)))), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Y Component Of((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition))), <=, 1.5)));
                Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
            Else If(And((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Compare(Distance Between(Divide(Add((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), 2), Event Player), >, Multiply(Add(Distance Between((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), 4), 1.2))));
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
            End;
            If(Or(Not((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), Compare(Magnitude Of(Velocity Of(Event Player)), >=, 3)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, Null);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False);
            Else If(Not((Event Player).pfSlowSince));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, Total Time Elapsed);
            Else If(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).pfSlowSince), >, 2.1));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, True);
            End;
            Wait(0.064, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core】Stop throttle when pfDestination is reset") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfIsPathfinding == True;
        (Event Player).pfDestination == Null;
    }
    actions {
        Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsPathfinding, False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSavedTarget, Null);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1);
    }
}

rule ("【PF Core】Insurance") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfIsPathfinding == True;
        (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(And(And(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, (Event Player).pfGoalNode), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget), <, Distance Between((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget))), Is In Line of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, Empty Array)), Add((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, Empty Array)), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
            Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
        Else If(And(And(And(Compare((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, !=, (Event Player).pfDestination), Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1)), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, (Event Player).pfDestination), <, 5)), Is In Line of Sight(Add((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, Up), Add((Event Player).pfDestination, Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, (Event Player).pfDestination);
        Else If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), >, 0.4), Not(Or(Not((Event Player).pfSavedTarget), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfDestination, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget), >, 5)))), Or((Event Player).pfIsStuck, Compare(Subtract(Distance Between((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), Multiply(4.95, Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, True)))), <, Null))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Rooted))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned))));
            If(Compare(Subtract(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 3), 2), ==, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex));
                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0} struggled to reach node {1} at {2}. Try adjusting your map in the editor.", Event Player, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex)));
                Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition);
            Else;
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 3, Add((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 2));
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
            End;
        End;
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 119, Array(Color(Red), Color(Blue), Color(Green), Color(Orange)));

        Set Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_State, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, False, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(z, Empty Array);
    }
}
