rule ("destroy door effects") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        DestroyDoorEffects;
    }
    actions {
        Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamA, Global.doorIndex), ==, Null), True);
        Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamA, Global.doorIndex));
        //__label_if_35__:
        Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamB, Global.doorIndex), ==, Null), True);
        Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamB, Global.doorIndex));
        //__label_if_36__:
        Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamA, Global.doorIndex, Null);
        Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamB, Global.doorIndex, Null);
    }
}

rule ("Collision Logic") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        Global.start == 1;
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 46), ==, 1));
            Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 46), ==, Null), 999999995904);
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        If(Compare((Event Player).outofpsawn, ==, Null));
            Wait Until(Compare((Event Player).outofpsawn, ==, 1), 999999995904);
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Divide(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Position Of(Event Player)), 2));
        Wait(Add(Add(0.16, Divide(Multiply(0.32, Server Load Peak), 255)), Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.016)), Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Max(Server Load Average, Server Load), <, 245), 0.5);
        If(Or(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).lastsavedpos, Event Player), >, 40), Compare((Event Player).doorSkipCheck, ==, True)));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck, False);
            Loop If Condition Is True;
            Abort;
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestwall, Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Index), Or(Or(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Event Player), <=, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Element))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).active_wall, Current Array Element), ==, 1)), Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Current Array Element)), >, Null), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Event Player), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Current Array Element)), >, Null)))));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, Null);
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 11), <, Count Of((Event Player).closestwall)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, Null));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, Null);
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
            End;
            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array((Event Player).closestwall, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 11)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, 1));
                If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array))))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z));
                Else If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array))))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z));
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Position Of(Event Player));
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos, Vector(X Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player)), Y Component Of((Event Player).closestbodypos), Z Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player))));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos, Add((Event Player).fullbodypos, Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z), Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z))), Dot Product(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)))));
                If(Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), >, Null), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), >, Null)));
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 12, Divide(Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), Dot Product(Direction Towards((Event Player).lastsavedpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z))));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection, Add((Event Player).fullbodypos, Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).lastsavedpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Vector(True, True, True)), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 12))));
                    If(And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.z)), Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of((Event Player).prevpos_intersection), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z)))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z))), 2))));
                        Cancel Primary Action(Event Player);
                        Teleport(Event Player, Add((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Vector(True, Empty Array, True)), 2)));
                    End;
                End;
                If(And(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).fullbodypos, (Event Player).filterpos), <=, 4), And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract((Event Player).filterpos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.z)), Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of((Event Player).filterpos), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z)))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z))), 2)))));
                    If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).active_wall, Global.z), ==, False));
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, 1);
                        Skip If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, True), !=, False), True);
                        Set Gravity(Event Player, 100);
                        //__label_if_37__:
                    End;
                    Apply Impulse(Event Player, Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).filterpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Vector(True, Empty Array, True)), 1, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion);
                    Skip If(Compare((Event Player).doorNotif, !=, Null), True);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 1);
                    //__label_if_38__:
                    If(And(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)), Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global.costmultiplier), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 3))), (Event Player).creditcardlimit))));
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), 50);
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Lúcio Sound Barrier Cast Sound, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), 100);
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global.costmultiplier), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 3))), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 80, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Zone, Global.z));
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 79, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 80), ==, -1), Array(Global.z), Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Index), Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Zone, Current Array Element), ==, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 80)))));
                        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 79)), True);
                            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 79), Global.i));
                            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, 1));
                                Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global.z);
                                Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects);
                                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_State, Global.z, Null);
                                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, Null);
                                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 56, Append To Array, Add(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 3), Global.z));
                            End;
                        End;
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Zone bought!"));
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, Null);
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 1000, Destination and Rate);
                        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                        Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
                    End;
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, Subtract(100, Multiply(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Event Player), Event Player, Rotation))), Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).filterpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), -1)), 100)));
                Else;
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, Null);
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100);
                End;
            End;
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, Add, True);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("Reset door notif") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).doorNotif == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold {0} to buy zone - cost: {1}", Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global.costmultiplier), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 3))), To Nearest)));
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, Null);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("make the door effects") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        makedoors;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 2), ==, 1), 99999);
        Set Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer, Null);
        While(Compare(Global.dooreffectsequencer, <, Count Of(Global.allDoors_Corner)));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.dooreffectsequencer), ==, 1));
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer)), Color(Red), Visible To);
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamA, Global.dooreffectsequencer, Last Created Entity);
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Vector(Empty Array, Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer)), Empty Array)), Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Vector(True, Empty Array, True))), Color(Red), Visible To);
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamB, Global.dooreffectsequencer, Last Created Entity);
                Skip If(Modulo(Global.dooreffectsequencer, 3), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_39__:
            End;
            Modify Global Variable(dooreffectsequencer, Add, True);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("draw the doors") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.doorsNeedDrawing == True;
    }
    actions {
        Call Subroutine(makedoors);
        Set Global Variable(doorsNeedDrawing, False);
    }
}

rule ("Reset") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Count Of(Remove From Array((Event Player).active_wall, Null)) == Null;
    }
    actions {
        Enable Movement Collision With Environment(Event Player);
    }
}

rule ("anticrash: slow motion") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Min(If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Server Load, >=, 150)), Max(93, Round To Integer(Subtract(100, Divide(Multiply(7, Subtract(Server Load, 150)), 105)), Up)), 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 42), 50, 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Global.slowmoCutscene, Global.slowmoCutscene, 100), If-Then-Else(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 44), Compare(Global.start, ==, 1)), 10, 100)))) != Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 43);
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 43, Min(If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Server Load, >=, 150)), Max(93, Round To Integer(Subtract(100, Divide(Multiply(7, Subtract(Server Load, 150)), 105)), Up)), 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 42), 50, 100), Min(If-Then-Else(Global.slowmoCutscene, Global.slowmoCutscene, 100), If-Then-Else(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 44), Compare(Global.start, ==, 1)), 10, 100)))));
        Set Slow Motion(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 43));
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("anticrash: emergency slow motion") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
        Server Load >= 250;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 44, True);
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Server overloaded: slowing down time"));
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 200), 3);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 44, False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("68 player joined invincible") {
    event {
        Player Joined Match;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Phased Out, 30);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, (Event Player).points);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, cashback, 1);
    }
}

rule ("120  Remove bots when players join") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 2));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 14, Compare(Global.botRemoveSlot, <=, 17), True);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.botRemoveSlot);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 3));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 10, Compare(Global.botRemoveSlot, <=, 17), True);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.botRemoveSlot);
            End;
        Else If(Compare(Number Of Players(Team 1), ==, 4));
            For Global Variable(botRemoveSlot, 8, Compare(Global.botRemoveSlot, <=, 17), True);
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.botRemoveSlot);
            End;
    }
}

rule ("Subroutine decompressNSub") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        decompressNSub;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(decodeString, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3)), True);
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, Null);
            While(Compare(Global.decodePosition, <, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString))));
                Modify Global Variable(decodeOut, Append To Array, Divide(Add(Add(Add(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Global.decodePosition)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 1), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, True))), 1024), Null)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 2), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, 2))), 1048576), Null)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 2)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, True)));
                Skip If(Modulo(Count Of(Global.decodeOut), 20), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_40__:
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, First Of(Global.decodeIn));
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("Subroutine decompressVSub") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        decompressVSub;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(decodeString, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3)), True);
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, Null);
            While(Compare(Global.decodePosition, <, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString))));
                Modify Global Variable(decodeOut, Append To Array, Vector(Divide(Add(Add(Add(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Global.decodePosition)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 1), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, True))), 1024), Null)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 2), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, 2))), 1048576), Null)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 2)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, True)), Divide(Add(Add(Add(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, First Of(Global.decodeIn)))), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 1), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Add(Global.decodePosition, First Of(Global.decodeIn)), True))), 1024), Null)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 2), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Add(Global.decodePosition, First Of(Global.decodeIn)), 2))), 1048576), Null)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 2)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, True)), Divide(Add(Add(Add(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Global.decodePosition, Multiply(2, First Of(Global.decodeIn))))), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 1), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Add(Global.decodePosition, Multiply(2, First Of(Global.decodeIn))), True))), 1024), Null)), If-Then-Else(Compare(First Of(Global.decodeIn), >, 2), Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 3), Global.decodeString), Add(Add(Global.decodePosition, Multiply(2, First Of(Global.decodeIn))), 2))), 1048576), Null)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, 2)), Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, True))));
                Skip If(Modulo(Count Of(Global.decodeOut), 20), True);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
                //__label_if_41__:
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, Multiply(3, First Of(Global.decodeIn)));
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("488 button held teleporter") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Count Of(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 3, Team 1, Off)) > Null;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 3, Team 1, Off), And(Is Alive(Current Array Element), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))))));
            If(Compare(Global.teleporterwait, ==, False));
                Set Global Variable(teleporterwait, True);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array);
                Create Effect(Event Player, Ana Nano Boosted Effect, Team Of(Event Player), Event Player, True, Visible To Position and Radius);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Winston Tesla Cannon Target Effect, Color(Black), Event Player, 80, Visible To Position and Radius);
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 5, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Stunned, 0.25);
                Destroy Effect(First Of((Event Player).teleporterEffects));
                Play Effect(Local Player, Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 80);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Kill(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 5, Team 2, Off), First Of(All Living Players(Team 1)));
                Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player).teleporterEffects, True));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 120, Color(Red));
                If(Compare(Count Of(Global.teleporterpositions), >, Null));
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, False);
                    Set Global Variable(telespot, Modulo(Add(Global.telespot, True), Count Of(Global.teleporterpositions)));
                    Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, 1);
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Null);
                    Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
                    Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 136), ==, True), 15);
                    Teleport(All Players(Team 1), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot));
                    For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, True);
                        If(And(Is Dead(Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.Died, Global.forindex), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500)), >, 1)));
                            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Nearest Walkable Position(Add(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Vector(Subtract(Multiply(Global.forindex, 1.5), 2.25), Empty Array, 2))));
                        End;
                    End;
                    Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, Null, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(Null, 1, 2), Global.telespot), True)));
                    Call Subroutine(zone1);
                    Skip(3);
                    Call Subroutine(zone2);
                    Skip(True);
                    Call Subroutine(zone3);
                    //lbl_0:
                    //lbl_1:
                End;
                Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, Null);
                Set Global Variable(doorsNeedDrawing, True);
    }
}

rule ("wave boss tables") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 110, Array(Hero(Doomfist), Hero(Zenyatta), Hero(Roadhog)));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 111, Array(Button(Ability 1), Button(Ability 2), Button(Secondary Fire), Button(Ultimate), Button(Melee)));
    }
}

rule ("wave boss dispatch") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SmDispatch;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 11));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, (Event Player).smCode);
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 6));
            If(Compare((Event Player).smHolding, ==, False));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 31, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 111), Subtract((Event Player).smCode, 6)));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 32, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 30));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding, True);
            End;
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 1));
            Press Button(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 111), Subtract((Event Player).smCode, True)));
        End;
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: find my row") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smRow, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global.bossBots, Event Player), Null, Add(Index Of Array Value(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 110), Hero Of(Event Player)), True)));
        If(Compare((Event Player).smRow, >, Null));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 113), Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True)));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Null);
            Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: timed sequence") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
        (Event Player).smRow > Null;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, Null);
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 28), <, 6));
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry, Multiply(Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True), 6), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 28)), 5));
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 29, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 10));
            If(And(And(Compare(Global.Difficulty, >=, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 4))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 29), >=, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 2)))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 29), <, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 3)))));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Global.smActions, (Event Player).smEntry));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, True)));
                Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
            End;
            Wait(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 114), Subtract(Global.Difficulty, True)), Abort When False);
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, Add, True);
        End;
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: health tri­gger") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smRow > Null;
        Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True), 3)) > Null;
        Normalized Health(Event Player) <= Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True), 3));
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True), 3), True)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, True), 3), 2)));
        Call Subroutine(SmDispatch);
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: hold button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smHolding == True;
    }
    actions {
        Start Holding Button(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 31));
        Wait(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 32), Ignore Condition);
        Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 31));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding, False);
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: special 11 ground slam") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smSpecial == 11;
    }
    actions {
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Event Player, 8);
        Damage(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 8, Team 1, Off), Event Player, 40);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, Null);
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: special 12 crouch with full ultimate") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smSpecial == 12;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Crouch));
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, Null);
    }
}

rule ("build the zone's doors") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        BuildZoneDoors;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, Null);
        For Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.allDoors_Corner), True);
            Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global.doorBuildIndex);
            Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), active_wall, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 3, Value In Array(Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 71), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 73), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 75)), Global.telespot));
        Call Subroutine(LoadZoneDoorData);
        Set Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, Null);
        Set Global Variable(looping, Null);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_State, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Empty Array);
        While(Compare(Global.doorBuildIndex, <, Count Of(Global.doorVectors)));
            Skip If(Modulo(Global.doorBuildIndex, 2), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_42__:
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 180), 0)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 90), 0)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_State, Append To Array, Array(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 56), Add(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 3), Global.doorBuildIndex)), Null, 1)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 57), Global.doorBuildIndex)));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Append To Array, Array(Add(Multiply(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 90), 0), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping)), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, True))))));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Append To Array, Array(Null));
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 3)), ==, 1));
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Width, Global.doorBuildIndex, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 4)));
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex, Add(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 4)))), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 5)))));
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 6);
            Else;
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex, Add(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping))), Vector(Empty Array, 50, Empty Array)));
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 4);
            End;
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Center, Global.doorBuildIndex, Divide(Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex)), 2));
            Modify Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, Add, True);
        End;
        Set Global Variable(doorVectors, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(doorParams, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 57, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, 1);
    }
}

rule ("zone loading: set up the team's zone") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.mapDataLoaded == True;
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 134) != Global.telespot;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(zoneLoading, True);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 134, Global.telespot);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 138, And(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8), !=, Null), Is Alive(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8))));
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, Null);
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139), <, 12));
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139), Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139));
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, Add, True);
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, True);
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Null);
        Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects);
        Call Subroutine(BuildZoneDoors);
        Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(And(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 135), ==, False), Compare(Global.doorsNeedDrawing, ==, False)), 30);
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False);
        Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, Null);
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 134), ==, Null));
            Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, Array(String Split(Custom String("޴୐޼ૼ޵׋޳̶޼૷޴૱ް੟޼ૢ޵˷ްֻ޼ࡿ޶ُ޲̕޼ࢀ޶ા޳ֵ޼ࡿ޶¿޵̊޼ଁ޶̔޵׋޼૥޶ி޵੔޼૥޷޵ૈ޼૥޷࣎޳ெ޼ࢁ޶࢔޲૚޼ु޷ͤ޳̃޼ु޷ੇްً޼޳޷ؚް޼ޯ޸ް|޼ु޸॥ް޶޼ु޸੐ް؀޼਺޻҈޲̳޼৽޻௎޲৅޼਒޼ࣳ޳޼৽޻{0}", Custom String("ோ0޲৉޼਒޻؎ް׏޼਻޼૝ޯ॥޼਺޼¤ޯ੅޼਺޻࡫ް˹޼ੇ޻ிްী޻௎޼޴̌޼:޼]޴̨޼:޻ؑ޵̘޼:޻؍޵̔޼:޼ࠟ޴͢޼:޼ࢀ޴͈޼͆޺ࢎ޵ࠦ޼ޯ޺؎޶׵޼ޯ޺ொ޶׽޼ޯ޺׆޶঳޼ޯ޺΋޶࣢޼ޯ޹ؘ޵ۘ޼ޯ޹֎޵؄޼ޯ޹੍޳֤޼ޯ޹਀޳҃޼{0}", Custom String("ޯ޸܊0޲ީ޼ޯ޸਻޲࡫޼ޯ޹੶޲؎޼ّ޺ࢎ޲ۘ޼֣޻ׄްؒ޻௎޻ִް؋޻௎޻਒ް˕޼ੇ޼ްِ޻௎޼΃ް~޼਺޻୾޵߾޼ࡿ޸͓޶ֹ޼ु޸՝޶֓޼஽޹϶޴֠޼ࡿ޷ீ޲՘޼ޯ޹;޲΅޼৙޹ૉް࠳޼਻޹ூްި޼਻޾˷޲֣޼৙޾̖޲֓޼ޯ޾਒ް޾޼ु޿͏޲{0}", Custom String("ַ޼ޯ޿΢0޳΢޼ޯ޿֞޳͍޼ޯ޾͊޲࡚޼ޯ޾؊޵׶޼ޯ޾̼޵ޫ޼ޯ޽ޮ޴֪޼͆޽ؚ޳޼ّ޽࠮޲ٔ޼ّ޽߱޲۠޼֣޼ंް݈޻்޼৅޳͗޼ّ޺ࡴ޶֨޼ࠩ޻஻޴ࣶ޼ࠫ޻௒޶֙޼ޯ޽޶۟޼ޯ޽ঁ޶ુ޼ޯ޾̩޶̷޼ޯ޾঴޵۠޼ޯ޾ু޵܇޼ࡿ޿े޶ֲ޼ु޿{0}", Custom String("ऺ޶׭޼஽޾ૈ0޴˅޼ࡿ߫«޴̔޼ࢀ߬਑޲઱޼ु߫ॗ޳˰޼ु͔߫޵̡޼૿߬઱޴૓޼૷߭ે޳΅޼૵߮̔ް੣޼૞͎߮ްֽ޼ࡿ߬ࢅ޲޼ࢁ̢߬ްؐ޼ޯؙ߫ް ޼ޯ޿૳ް޼ु޿ͦ޳֠޼ࡿ߬ૅ޵֧޼૥߬±޵੥޼૥߫ୀ޵૗޼૥߫֬޶־޻ெ޼ࣣ޶ࣿ޻௕޼ ޷ͦ޻{0}", Custom String("଩޼#޶׵޻ி޻ٗ0޶؅޼ ޺਑޷࠷޼˜޺ं޷܋޼֣޹઻޶৓޼ޯ޹ૈ")))))), First Of(Null)), String Split(Custom String("# (  ! $ '  # %  $ &  % ' /  & # ( ,  ' ! )  ( *  ) +  *  ' -   , .  -  & :  / ;  : <  ;    > _   = ? rule ("zone loading: bring the zombies back") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 137) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 137, False);
        If(And(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Global.numberOfZombies, >=, 1)), Compare(Global.Bosssequencer, ==, Null)));
            Call Subroutine(setbotamount);
            Modify Global Variable(botamount, Min, Subtract(Global.numberOfZombies, If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global.spawntype, !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 138), ==, True)), 1, Null)));
            If(And(And(Compare(Global.spawntype, !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 138), ==, True)), Compare(Players In Slot(False, Team 2), ==, Null)));
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 7), Global.g), Team 2, False, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Players In Slot(False, Team 2));
                Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
            End;
            Set Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), Null, 1));
            While(And(Compare(Global.zoneRefillSlot, <=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), Subtract(Global.botamount, True), Global.botamount)), Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False)));
                If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.zoneRefillSlot, Team 2), ==, Null));
                    Create Dummy Bot(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(False, 37)), If-Then-Else(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneRefillSlot, 2), ==, 1), Value In Array(Global.bot1spawn, Global.g), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 15), Global.g))), Team 2, Global.zoneRefillSlot, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Null);
                    Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global.zoneRefillSlot, Global.zoneRefillSlot);
                    Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
                End;
                Modify Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, Add, True);
            End;
    }
}

rule ("all the effects") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(buyperks, 8, Vector(240.047, 46.147, 174.277));
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, True);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Good Aura, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 120), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 120), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color);
        End;
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, True);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), 4, Visible To Position and Radius);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sphere, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), (Players In Slot(Evaluate Once(Global.forindex), Team 1)).sea, Visible To Position and Radius);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("{0}", Evaluate Once(Random Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 61)))), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), 3, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
        End;
        Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Global.rngbox, 2, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 25, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 40, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Purple), Default Visibility);
        Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Color(Yellow), Global.rngbox, 2, Visible To Position and Radius);
        Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Symmetra Photon Projector, Null, Add(Up, Global.rngbox), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
    }
}

rule ("clear zone markers") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        ClearZoneMarkers;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.zoneEffects), True);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.zoneEffects, Global.zoneMarkerIndex));
            Skip If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), !=, 9), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_43__:
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.zoneTexts), True);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Global.zoneTexts, Global.zoneMarkerIndex));
            Skip If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), !=, 9), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_44__:
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 129)), True);
            Destroy Icon(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 129), Global.zoneMarkerIndex));
            Skip If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), !=, 9), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_45__:
        End;
        Set Global Variable(zoneEffects, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(zoneTexts, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Null);
    }
}

rule ("draw zone markers") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        DrawZoneMarkers;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 7, True);
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), True, Visible To and Color);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                If(Compare(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, !=, 6));
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    Create In-World Text(Local Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 143), Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), If-Then-Else(Compare(Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex), ==, Null), 3.25, 2.25), Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
                Else;
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Zarya Graviton, Null, Add(Vector(Empty Array, 1.5, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), Up, 0.05, Visible To Friendly To);
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                    If(Compare(Global.poweron, ==, Null));
                        Create Icon(All Living Players(Team 1), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 6)), Bolt, Visible To and Position, Color(Yellow), True);
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Last Created Entity);
                    End;
                End;
            End;
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        End;
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8), True, Visible To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Ability Icon String(Hero(Ashe), Button(Ability 2)), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8)), 3.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.buyheros), True);
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), !=, Null)));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Yellow), Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), 2, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(Blue), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            End;
            Skip If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 6), !=, 5), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_46__:
        End;
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.bossbuypositions), True);
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.bossbuypositions, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Spawn boss: {0} : 15000 points", Input Binding String(Button(Interact))), Add(Value In Array(Global.bossbuypositions, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(White), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.bossbuypositions, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), True, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
        End;
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(First Of(Global.bankButtons), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
            For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 4, True);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Value In Array(Array(Color(Red), Color(Red), Color(Red), Color(Yellow)), Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Value In Array(Array(1.5, 1, 1, 1), Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
            For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 4, True);
                Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Add(First Of(Global.bankButtons), Value In Array(Array(Vector(Empty Array, 0.25, 0.7), Vector(Empty Array, -0.5, Empty Array), Vector(0.5, -0.5, Empty Array), Vector(-0.5, -0.5, Empty Array)), Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Value In Array(Array(Backward, Up, Up, Up), Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Value In Array(Array(0.05, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1), Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Visible To Friendly To);
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            End;
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Property damage: ${0}", Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 47)), Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 3), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Yellow), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Bank: {0} Points", Global.bankPoints), Add(First Of(Global.bankButtons), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Arrow: Up), Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, True), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Arrow: Down), Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 2), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Blue), Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4), True, Visible To Position and Radius);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Ability Icon String(Hero(Kiriko), Button(Ability 1)), Add(Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Zarya Particle Cannon, Null, Add(Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4), Vector(Empty Array, 0.5, Empty Array)), Up, 0.03, Visible To Friendly To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Add(Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4), Vector(Empty Array, 0.5, Empty Array)), Down, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
        End;
        If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Vector(-101.303, -150.36, -17.831), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot));
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("Bastion Trap: press {0} {1}", Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), String("{0}: {1}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Bastion)), 1000)), Vector(-83.763, -158.95, 64.107), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Purple), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Vector(-101.303, -150.36, -17.831), 2, Visible To Position and Radius);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("The end : 50,000 points"), Vector(-101.303, -150.36, -17.831), True, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("zone markers") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 128) != Global.telespot;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 128, Global.telespot);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 135, True);
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 136), ==, True), 15);
        Call Subroutine(ClearZoneMarkers);
        Call Subroutine(DrawZoneMarkers);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 135, False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("fire sale boxes") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
        Global.fireSale == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 132, Global.telespot);
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.mysterylocations), True);
            Skip If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), Current Array Element)))), !=, Global.telespot), True);
            Modify Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex));
            //__label_if_47__:
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot), Compare(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), !=, Global.rngbox)));
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex)), 2, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Orange), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex))), True, Visible To);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex))), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(Orange), Default Visibility);
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
            End;
        End;
        Wait Until(Or(Compare(Global.fireSale, !=, True), Compare(Global.telespot, !=, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 132))), 99999);
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 130)), True);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 130), Global.fireSaleIndex));
        End;
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 131)), True);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 131), Global.fireSaleIndex));
        End;
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("202 Purchase hero message") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 18) == False;
        Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Global.buyheros), True);
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.i)), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.i), !=, Null)));
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i);
                Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Press: {1} for Hero = {2}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).messageSpot)), Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Custom String("{0} Points -- Hold: {1} for follower = {2}", Round To Integer(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).messageSpot), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest), Input Binding String(Button(Reload)), Custom String("{0} Points --- Ammo cost {1}", Round To Integer(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).messageSpot), 3), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest), Round To Integer(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).messageSpot), 2), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest)))));
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 18, True);
                Wait(5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 18, False);
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("Purchase perk machine message") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).perkBuyMessTimer == False;
        (Event Player).outofpsawn == 1;
        Is True For Any(Array Slice(Global.buyperks, False, 7), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition);
        For Global Variable(i, 0, 7, True);
            If(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Global.i)), <, 2));
                If(And(Compare(Global.poweron, ==, Null), Compare(Global.i, ==, 6)));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Dont you dare touch that!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                Else If(Compare(Global.poweron, ==, Null));
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} What a Mysterious Machine for my Trials!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
                Else If(Compare(Global.i, !=, 6));
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i);
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Press: {1} for Perk = {2}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Input Binding String(Button(Interact)), Custom String("{0} == {1} Points", Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 142), (Event Player).messageSpot), Round To Integer(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, (Event Player).messageSpot), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest))));
                End;
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkBuyMessTimer, True);
                Wait(5, Ignore Condition);
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkBuyMessTimer, False);
                Break;
            End;
        End;
    }
}

rule ("info UI cancel") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start > Null;
    }
    actions {
        Skip If(Compare(Count Of((Event Player).InfoUI), ==, False), 5);
        Destroy HUD Text(Value In Array((Event Player).InfoUI, Subtract(Count Of((Event Player).InfoUI), True)));
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI, Remove From Array By Index, Subtract(Count Of((Event Player).InfoUI), True));
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Loop If(Compare(Count Of((Event Player).InfoUI), !=, Null));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 5));
        //lbl_0:
        Abort;
    }
}

    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == False;
    }
    actions {
        For Player Variable(Event Player, infoUIIndex, 0, 18, True);
            Skip If(Compare((Event Player).infoUIIndex, <=, Null), True);
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            //__label_if_48__:

                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsViewers, ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Null, Custom String("{0} {1}", Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Array(Icon String(Checkmark), Icon String(Bolt), Icon String(Diamond), Icon String(Heart)), Index Of Array Value(Array(Null, 7, 10, 14), (Event Player).infoUIIndex))), Value In Array(Global.infoText, Evaluate Once((Event Player).infoUIIndex))), Left, Add(10, (Event Player).infoUIIndex), Null, Null, Color(Yellow), Visible To and String, Default Visibility);
            Else If(Compare((Event Player).infoUIIndex, ==, 5));
                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsViewers, ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Null, Value In Array(Global.infoText, 5), Left, 15, Null, Null, Color(Green), Visible To and String, Default Visibility);
            Else;
                Create HUD Text(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsViewers, ==, Null), Event Player, Null), Null, Value In Array(Global.infoText, Evaluate Once((Event Player).infoUIIndex)), Null, Left, Add(10, (Event Player).infoUIIndex), Null, Color(White), Null, Visible To and String, Default Visibility);
            End;
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, InfoUI, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, Add(Total Time Elapsed, 0.16));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 49, True);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 35, 1);
    }
}

rule ("toggle perk info") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).perkinfo == False;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Jump)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkinfo, 1);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkinfo, 2);
        Wait(7, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkinfo, 1);
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, perkinfo, False);
    }
}

rule ("settings menu: open and close with Melee") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == False;
        Global.textListsLoaded == True;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Melee)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(settingsViewers, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsViewers, ==, Null), All Players(Team 1), Null));
        Start Camera(Global.settingsViewers, First Of(Global.settingsCamera), Value In Array(Global.settingsCamera, True), 0);
        Set Facing(Global.settingsViewers, Direction Towards(First Of(Global.settingsCamera), Value In Array(Global.settingsCamera, True)), To World);
    }
}

rule ("settings menu: show it") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == False;
        Global.textListsLoaded == True;
        Global.mapDataLoaded == True;
    }
    actions {
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
        Create In-World Text(Global.settingsViewers, Custom String("+"), Add(First Of(Global.settingsCamera), Multiply(Facing Direction Of(Local Player), 12)), 3, Do Not Clip, Visible To Position and String, Color(Yellow), Default Visibility);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 133, Array(Last Text ID));
        For Global Variable(settingsRow, 0, 9, True);
            Create In-World Text(Global.settingsViewers, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 10), Value In Array(Array(Null, 1, Add(3, Global.Difficulty), Add(9, Global.spawntype), Add(14, If-Then-Else(Global.experiencesystem, 1, Null)), 2, Add(14, Multiply(2, Global.Difficulty)), Add(15, Multiply(2, Global.Difficulty)), 3), Evaluate Once(Global.settingsRow))), Add(Value In Array(Global.settingsCamera, True), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.settingsCamera, 2), Subtract(3, Multiply(Global.settingsRow, 0.75)))), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsRow, ==, Null), 3, If-Then-Else(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Global.settingsRow, 3.5)), <, 2), 2.2, 1.3)), Do Not Clip, Visible To and String, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.settingsRow, ==, Null), Color(Yellow), If-Then-Else(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Global.settingsRow, 3.5)), <, 2), Color(Orange), Color(White))), Default Visibility);
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 133, Append To Array, Last Text ID);
        End;
        Wait Until(Compare(Global.start, !=, False), 99999);
        For Global Variable(settingsRow, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 133)), True);
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 133), Global.settingsRow));
        End;
    }
}

rule ("settings menu: click a setting") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.settingsViewers != Null;
        Or(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire))) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 42, If-Then-Else(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), 1, -1));
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, menuPick, Round To Integer(Subtract(2, Multiply(16, Dot Product(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Value In Array(Global.settingsCamera, 2)))), To Nearest));
        If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, Null));
            Set Global Variable(Difficulty, Max(True, Min(5, Add(Global.Difficulty, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 42)))));
            Set Global Variable(difficultyStats, Array Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 30), Subtract(Multiply(Global.Difficulty, 6), 6), 6));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 1));
            Set Global Variable(spawntype, Modulo(Add(Add(Global.spawntype, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 42)), 5), 5));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 2));
            Set Global Variable(experiencesystem, Not(Global.experiencesystem));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 3));
            Set Global Variable(settingsViewers, Null);
        End;
        Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 40);
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
    }
}

rule ("set dialogue text") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1;
    }
    actions {
