            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 123, First Of(Value In Array(Global.dialogueLines, Global.startlines)));
        Else;
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 123, Custom String("{0}{1}{2}", First Of(Value In Array(Global.dialogueLines, Global.startlines)), Random Integer(100, 9999), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.dialogueLines, Global.startlines), True)));
    }
}

rule ("Dialogue - Text scrolling Function") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 117) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Modify Global Variable(Character_Nr, Add, True);
        Skip If(Modulo(Global.Character_Nr, 3), True);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sombra Translocator Reappear Sound, First Of(All Players(All Teams)), Eye Position(First Of(All Players(All Teams))), 100);
        //__label_if_49__:
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(String Split(Custom String(".0!0?0-"), First Of(Null)), Char In String(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 123), Global.Character_Nr)), 0.35, 0.05), Ignore Condition);
        Wait Until(Compare(Global.Character_Nr, <=, String Length(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 123))), 99999);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("boss buy") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
        Is True For Any(Global.bossbuypositions, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3);
        If(Compare(Global.Bosssequencer, !=, Null));
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} One champion at a time!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)));
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest));
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1);
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins);
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 15000, Destination and Rate);
            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70);
            Set Global Variable(bossSpot, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.bossbuypositions, Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))));
            Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, Add(Index Of Array Value(Global.bossbuypositions, Global.bossSpot), True));
        Else;
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You cannot afford to face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
    }
}

rule ("spawn boss parts") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SpawnBossParts;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.bossPartList), True);
            Create Dummy Bot(First Of(Value In Array(Global.bossPartList, Global.bossIndex)), Team 2, -1, Global.bossSpot, Null);
            Modify Global Variable(bossBots, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
            Set Player Variable(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Part, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.bossPartList, Global.bossIndex), 2));
            Start Scaling Player(Last Of(Global.bossBots), 0.7, True);
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
            Attach Players(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Global.bossBody, Multiply(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.bossPartList, Global.bossIndex), True), 0.7));
            Start Facing(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Facing Direction Of(Global.bossBody), 1000, To World, Direction and Turn Rate);
            Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        End;
    }
}

rule ("spawn boss 1: Devastator") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SpawnDevastator;
    }
    actions {
        Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Orisa), Team 2, -1, Global.bossSpot, Null);
        Set Global Variable(bossBody, Last Created Entity);
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Array(Global.bossBody));
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition);
        Set Damage Dealt(Global.bossBody, 200);
        Set Knockback Received(Global.bossBody, 0);
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global.bossBody, _extendedPlayerCollection, 39, 100);
        Start Scaling Player(Global.bossBody, 2.1, True);
        Set Max Health(Global.bossBody, 1000);
        Start Forcing Dummy Bot Name(Global.bossBody, Custom String("Devastator"));
        Add Health Pool To Player(Global.bossBody, Armor, 3000, True, True);
        Add Health Pool To Player(Global.bossBody, Health, 3000, True, True);
        Set Player Variable(Global.bossBody, zombieBusy, True);
        Set Global Variable(bossPartList, Array(Array(Hero(Wrecking Ball), Vector(True, 3, True), 1), Array(Hero(Wrecking Ball), Vector(-1, 3, True), 1), Array(Hero(Wrecking Ball), Vector(Empty Array, 4, -1), 2), Array(Hero(Bastion), Vector(1.75, 4.25, Empty Array), 3), Array(Hero(Bastion), Vector(-1.75, 4.25, Empty Array), 3), Array(Hero(Wrecking Ball), Vector(Empty Array, 1.75, -1.25), 4)));
        Call Subroutine(SpawnBossParts);
    }
}

rule ("boss fight: clear the wave, then the boss comes") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.Bosssequencer != Null;
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 28), ==, False), 2);
        Set Global Variable(numberOfZombies, 1);
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Null);
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array);
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, 12, True);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.bossIndex, Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.bossIndex);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("A champion approaches!"));
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} You want a real fight? Face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra))));
        Wait(2, Abort When False);
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, Null, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(1, 2, 3), Global.Bosssequencer), True)));
        Call Subroutine(SpawnDevastator);
        Skip(3);
        Call Subroutine(SpawnColossalCharger);
        Skip(True);
        Call Subroutine(SpawnImperialWarmech);
        //lbl_0:
        //lbl_1:
    }
}

rule ("boss fight: the boss attacks players in range") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.Bosssequencer != Null;
        Event Player == Global.bossBody;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        If(And(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, !=, Null), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).zombieTarget), <, 25)));
            Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
        Else;
            Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire));
        End;
        Wait(0.5, Abort When False);
        Loop If Condition Is True;
    }
}

rule ("end boss fight") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        EndBossFight;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, 12, True);
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.bossIndex, Team 2), !=, Null));
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.bossIndex);
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause);
            End;
        End;
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.bossEffects), True);
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.bossEffects, Global.bossIndex));
        End;
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array);
        Set Global Variable(bossBody, Null);
        Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, Null);
    }
}

rule ("boss fight: the boss is defeated") {
    event {
        Player Died;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Event Player == Global.bossBody;
    }
    actions {
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("Champion defeated! +20,000 points"));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))), _extendedPlayerCollection, 43, True);
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
        Call Subroutine(EndBossFight);
        Set Global Variable(numberOfZombies, Null);
    }
}

        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), DVa Self Destruct Explosion Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(15, Null, 3, 5, 9), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(4, 1, 3, 2), (Event Player).Part), True)));
        Set Player Variable At Index(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), _extendedPlayerCollection, 39, False);
        Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null, Stunned, 2);
        Skip(12);
        Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null, Stunned, 2);
        Skip(10);
        Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null, Stunned, 2);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ashe Dynamite Explosion Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Ashe Dynamite Explosion Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
        Skip(6);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sombra EMP Explosion Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Sombra EMP Explosion Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
        Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null, Stunned, 4);
        Damage(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 5, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Attacker, 1500);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Junkrat RIP Tire Explosion Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1);
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Junkrat RIP Tire Explosion Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
        //lbl_0:
        //lbl_1:
        //lbl_2:
    }
}

rule ("277 Colossal charger dies") {
    event {
        Player Took Damage;
        Team 2;
        Reinhardt;
    }
    conditions {
        Health(Event Player) == 1;
        Global.Bosssequencer == 2;
    }
    actions {
        Set Status(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null, Stunned, 6);
        Wait(3, Ignore Condition);
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Knocked Down, 9999);
        Stop Forcing Throttle(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team Of(Event Player), Off));
        Detach Players(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team Of(Event Player), Off));
        Wait(True, Ignore Condition);
        Clear Status(Event Player, Unkillable);
        Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
        Kill(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 3, Team Of(Event Player), Off), Null);
    }
}

rule ("289 Imperial warmech set attacks") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        D.Va;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.Bosssequencer == 3;
        Event Player == Global.bossBody;
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)) == True;
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks, Random Integer(2, 6));
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition);
        If(Compare((Event Player).Major_Attacks, ==, 2));
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200);
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of(Event Player), 1);
            Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition);
        End;
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts, Array(Array(Hero(Pharah), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Zarya), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Sojourn), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Moira), Vector(Empty Array, 4, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Junkrat), Vector(Empty Array, 4, -1))));
        For Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex, 0, Count Of((Event Player).warmachAttackParts), True);
            If(Compare((Event Player).warmachAttackIndex, ==, Subtract((Event Player).Major_Attacks, 2)));
                Create Dummy Bot(First Of(Value In Array((Event Player).warmachAttackParts, (Event Player).warmachAttackIndex)), Team 2, -1, Event Player, Null);
                Modify Global Variable(bossBots, Append To Array, Last Created Entity);
                Wait(False, Ignore Condition);
                Set Status(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Null, Phased Out, 9999);
                Start Scaling Player(Last Of(Global.bossBots), 0.7, True);
                Attach Players(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Event Player, Multiply(Value In Array(Value In Array((Event Player).warmachAttackParts, (Event Player).warmachAttackIndex), True), 0.7));
                If(Compare((Event Player).Major_Attacks, ==, 3));
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Add(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Vector(Random Integer(-1, True), Empty Array, Empty Array)), 1000, To Player, Direction and Turn Rate);
                Else;
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), 1000, To World, Direction and Turn Rate);
                End;
                Wait(7, Ignore Condition);
                Loop If Condition Is True;
            End;
        End;
    }
}
